By the law of iterated expectations,

=c_{1+\alpha}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{t}^{(i)}\big|^{1+\alpha}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{t}^{(i)}\big|^{1+\alpha}\right]\bigg\}=c_{1+\alpha}2\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{t}^{(i)}\big|^{1+\alpha}\right]

Since
X_{t}^{(i)}\triangleq W_{1:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})
,

=c_{1+\alpha}2\mathbb{E}\left[\big|W_{1:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha}\right]

Since
W_{1:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\leq\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}
by Condition
2
(Minimum Exploration),

\leq c_{1+\alpha}2\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)(1+\alpha)}\mathbb{E}\left[\big|f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha}\right]<\infty.

The final inequality holds since
\mathbb{E}\left[\big|f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha}\right]<\infty
by assumption of this Theorem. We have now shown that property
(ii)
holds. We have now shown that display (
D.1
) holds.

Proving Display (
D.2
) holds.

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big\{\hat{\pi}_{2:t}^{(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\overset{P}{\to}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right].

(D.7)

Note that

\big\{\hat{\pi}_{2:t}^{(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})=W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}).

By Condition
2
,
\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}\leq\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}
a.s. Thus,
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha}\right]\\
\leq\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha}\right]<\infty
; the final inequality holds by the assumption that

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|f\big(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|^{1+\alpha}\right]<\infty
. Thus, using the result from display (
D.1
),

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big\{\hat{\pi}_{2:t}^{(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\\
\overset{P}{\to}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right].~~~\blacksquare

D.2
Weighted Martingale Triangular Array Uniform Law of Large Numbers (Theorem
D.2
)

Theorem D.2

(Weighted Martingale Triangular Array Uniform Weak Law of Large Numbers)
.

Let
\mathcal{F}
be a class of real-valued, measurable functions such that for some
\alpha>0
,
N_{[~]}\big(\epsilon,\mathcal{F},L_{1+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)<\infty
for any
\epsilon>0
.
Under Condition
2
(Minimum Exploration),

\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]\right\}\bigg|\overset{P}{\to}0.

(D.8)

Moreover,

\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\big\{\hat{\pi}_{2:t}^{(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]\right)\bigg|\overset{P}{\to}0.

(D.9)

Proof of Theorem
D.2
.

Showing display (
D.8
) holds.

Let
\epsilon>0
. For convenience, let
N_{\epsilon}\triangleq N_{[~]}\big(\epsilon,\mathcal{F},L_{1+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)
. Since
N_{[~]}\big(\epsilon,\mathcal{F},L_{1+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)<\infty
by assumption, we can finitely many brackets
\big\{(l_{k},u_{k})\big\}_{k=1}^{N_{\epsilon}}
that cover
\mathcal{F}
with
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha}\big]^{1/(1+\alpha)}\leq\epsilon
. Thus,

\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]\right\}

\leq\max_{k\in[1\colon N_{\epsilon}]}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]\right\}

By Theorem
D.1
(Weighted Martingale Triangular Array Weak Law of Large Numbers), for any
k\in[1\colon N_{\epsilon}]
,
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\overset{P}{\to}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]
. Since there are finitely many brackets, by Slutsky’s theorem this result holds simultaneously for all brackets
k\in[1\colon N_{\epsilon}]
.

=o_{P}(1)+\max_{k\in[1\colon N_{\epsilon}]}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]\right\}

By Jensen’s inequality

\leq o_{P}(1)+\max_{k\in[1\colon N_{\epsilon}]}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})|^{1+\alpha}\big]^{1/(1+\alpha)}\leq o_{P}(1)+\epsilon.

The last inequality above holds because our brackets were chosen to be at most of size
\epsilon
, in
L_{1+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})
norm. The above converges to zero because
\epsilon
can be chosen to be arbitrarily small.

The final result display (
D.8
) holds by using the same argument above to show that
\sup_{f\in\mathcal{F}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]-W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right\}\overset{P}{\to}0
.

Showing display (
D.9
) holds.

Note that

\big\{\hat{\pi}_{2:t}^{(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})=W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\\
=W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\bigg\{\prod_{t^{\prime}=2}^{t}\frac{1}{\pi_{t^{\prime}}(A_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)};\beta_{t^{\prime}-1}^{*})}\bigg\}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}).

By the result from display (
D.8
), to show display (
D.9
) holds it is sufficient to show that the function class

\mathcal{F}_{\pi}\triangleq\bigg\{\bigg(\prod_{t^{\prime}=2}^{t}\frac{1}{\pi_{t^{\prime}}(\,\cdot\,;\beta_{t^{\prime}-1}^{*})}\bigg)f(\,\cdot\,)\textnormal{~~s.t.~~}f\in\mathcal{F}\bigg\}

is such that
N_{[~]}\big(\epsilon,\mathcal{F}_{\pi},L_{1+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)<\infty
for all
\epsilon>0
.

Since
N_{\epsilon}\triangleq N_{[~]}\big(\epsilon,\mathcal{F},L_{1+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)<\infty
by conditions of the theorem, we can find brackets
\big\{(l_{k},u_{k})\big\}_{k=1}^{N_{\epsilon}}
that cover
\mathcal{F}
and
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha}\big]^{1/(1+\alpha)}\leq\epsilon
for each bracket
(l_{k},u_{k})
. Let
f\in\mathcal{F}
. We can find one of these brackets
(l_{k},u_{k})
such that
l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})
a.s.

Since
\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}>0
a.s., thus,

\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}).

So the brackets
\big\{\big(\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}l_{k},\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}u_{k}\big)\big\}_{k=1}^{N_{\epsilon}}
cover
\mathcal{F}_{\pi}
.

Moreover, since
\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}\leq\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}
a.s. by Condition
2
, thus

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha}\big]^{1/(1+\alpha)}\leq\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}\epsilon.

Thus, we have that
N_{[~]}\big(\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}\epsilon,\mathcal{F}_{W},L_{1+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)=N_{[~]}\big(\epsilon,\mathcal{F},L_{1+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)
. Thus, since
N_{[~]}\big(\epsilon,\mathcal{F},L_{1+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)<\infty
for any
\epsilon>0
(by assumption of the Theorem), thus we have that
N_{[~]}\big(\epsilon,\mathcal{F}_{W},L_{1+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)<\infty
for any
\epsilon>0
.
\blacksquare

D.3
Showing Terms are
O_{P}(1)
(Helper Lemma
D.3
)

Lemma D.3

(Showing Terms are
O_{P}(1)
(Helper Lemma))
.

Let
g
be a real-valued, function of
\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}
such that
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|\big]<\infty
. Under Condition
2
,
we have that
g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})=O_{P}(1)
.

Proof of Lemma
D.3
.

Let
\epsilon>0
. By Condition
2
(Minimum Exploration),

W_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big(\beta_{t^{\prime}-1}^{*},\hat{\beta}_{t^{\prime}-1}^{(n)}\big)=\frac{\pi_{t^{\prime}}^{*}(A_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})}{\hat{\pi}_{t^{\prime}}^{(n)}(A_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})}\geq\frac{\pi_{t^{\prime}}^{*}(A_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})}{1}\geq\pi_{\min}~~~\textnormal{a.s.}

By the above result and Markov inequality, for any
c_{\epsilon}>0
,

\mathbb{P}\big(\big|g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|>c_{\epsilon}\big)\leq c_{\epsilon}^{-1}\mathbb{E}\big[\big|g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|\big]\\
\leq c_{\epsilon}^{-1}\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}\mathbb{E}\left[W_{2:t}^{(i)}\big(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)}\big)\big|g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|\right]=c_{\epsilon}^{-1}\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|\right].

(D.10)

The above is less than or equal to
\epsilon
by choosing
c_{\epsilon}>\epsilon^{-1}\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|\big]
. This is sufficient by the definition of
O_{P}(1)
.
\blacksquare

D.4
Weighted Martingale Triangular Array Central Limit Theorem (Theorem
D.4
)

Theorem D.4

(Weighted Martingale Triangular Array Central Limit Theorem)
.

Let
f_{1},f_{2},...,f_{t}
be real-valued, measurable functions of
\mathcal{H}_{1}^{(i)},\mathcal{H}_{2}^{(i)},...,\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}
respectively such that

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t^{\prime}}^{*}}\left[\big|f_{t^{\prime}}\big(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big)\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]<\infty
for some
\alpha>0
, for each
t^{\prime}\in[1\colon t]
. We show that (below we use
W_{1}^{(i)}=1
)

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}W_{1:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\bigg]\bigg\}\\
\overset{D}{\to}\mathcal{N}\bigg(0,\textnormal{Var}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg(\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\bigg)\bigg)

(D.11)

for
\textnormal{Var}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left(\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\right)\triangleq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big\}^{2}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\right]^{2}
under the following conditions:

(A)

Conditions
2
(Minimum Exploration) and
3
(Lipschitz Policy Functions) hold.

(B)

\hat{\beta}_{t^{\prime}}^{(n)}-\beta_{t^{\prime}}^{*}=O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
for all
t^{\prime}\in[1\colon t-1]
.

Proof of Theorem
D.4
.
We want to show that display (
D.11
) holds for any
t\in[2\colon T]
. For notational convenience we consider the
t
set to
T
case; the argument holds by the same argument for any
t\in[2\colon T]
.

The first task is to rewrite the left-hand side of display (
D.11
) as a sum of triangular array martingale differences; we take an approach similar to that we used in the proof of Theorem
D.1
.

Note that the left-hand side of display (
D.11
) can be rewritten as follows:

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}W_{1:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\bigg]\bigg\}\\
=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bigg\{W_{1:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}\left[W_{1:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right]\bigg\}

Above we use
W_{1}^{(i)}=1
and
W_{1:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\triangleq W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})
.
Additionally, above the expectation
\mathbb{E}
is with respect to the data distribution used to collect the data, thus,
\mathbb{E}\left[W_{1:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right]=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right]
.

Let
X_{t}^{(i)}\triangleq W_{1:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})
and
X_{1:T}^{(i)}\triangleq\sum_{t=1}^{T}X_{t}^{(i)}
.

=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\left\{X_{t}^{(i)}-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{t}^{(i)}\right]\right\}=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{X_{1:T}^{(i)}-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\right]\right\}

Let
\mathcal{H}_{0}^{(1:n)}\triangleq\emptyset
and
S_{T+1}^{(1:n)}\triangleq\emptyset
. Note that
X_{1:T}^{(i)}=\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{T}^{(1:n)},S_{T+1}^{(1:n)}\right]
since
X_{1:T}^{(i)}
is known given
\mathcal{H}_{T}^{(1:n)}
. By telescoping series,

=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\underbrace{\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{0}^{(1:n)},S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\right]\right\}}_{\triangleq Z_{0}^{(i)}}\\
+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bigg[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\underbrace{\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\right\}}_{\triangleq Z_{t}^{(i)}}\bigg].

Note that the terms
Z_{t}^{(i)}
above are different from those we defined in the proof of Theorem
D.1
, since here the terms
X_{1:T}^{(i)}
are a sum over
T
terms.

Note that
\mathbb{E}\left[Z_{t}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]=0
for all
i\in[1\colon n]
and
t\in[1\colon T]
.
This is the case because for any
i\in[1\colon n]
and
t\in[1\colon T]
,

\mathbb{E}\left[Z_{t}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]

=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]=0.

The final equality above holds by the law of iterated expectations.

In the next two subsections we will show the following two results:

(i) Convergence of conditional variance

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(Z_{0}^{(i)}\big)^{2}\right]+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(Z_{t}^{(i)}\big)^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
\overset{P}{\to}\textnormal{Var}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\bigg(\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg).

(D.12)

(ii) Conditional Lindeberg

For any
\epsilon>0
,

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(Z_{0}^{(i)}\big)^{2}\mathbb{I}_{|Z_{0}^{(i)}|/\sqrt{n}>\epsilon}\right]+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(Z_{t}^{(i)}\big)^{2}\mathbb{I}_{|Z_{t}^{(i)}|/\sqrt{n}>\epsilon}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
\overset{P}{\to}0.

(D.13)

With the above two results we can apply Theorem 2.2 of
Dvoretzky 1972
(a martingale central limit theorem) to conclude that our desired result holds, i.e.,

\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{t=0}^{T}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Z_{t}^{(i)}\overset{D}{\to}\mathcal{N}\bigg(0,\textnormal{Var}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\bigg(\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg)\bigg).

We first show that display (
D.13
) holds. We then show that display (
D.12
) holds.

(ii) Conditional Lindeberg; Display (
D.13
).

For any
\epsilon>0
, we show that the following conditional Lindeberg term is
o_{P}(1)
:

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(Z_{0}^{(i)}\big)^{2}\mathbb{I}_{|Z_{0}^{(i)}|/\sqrt{n}>\epsilon}\right]+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(Z_{t}^{(i)}\big)^{2}\mathbb{I}_{|Z_{t}^{(i)}|/\sqrt{n}>\epsilon}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]

Note that for
\alpha>0
,

\mathbb{I}_{|Z|/\sqrt{n}>\epsilon}=\mathbb{I}_{|Z|/(\epsilon\sqrt{n})>1}=\mathbb{I}_{|Z|^{\alpha}/(\epsilon\sqrt{n})^{\alpha}>1}\leq|Z|^{\alpha}/(\epsilon\sqrt{n})^{\alpha}
.

Thus we can upper-bound the previous display as follows:

\leq\frac{1}{n(\epsilon\sqrt{n})^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big|Z_{0}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n(\epsilon\sqrt{n})^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big|Z_{t}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right].

(D.14)

Note that for any
t^{\prime}\in[0\colon T]
,

\big|Z_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}=\left|\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(1:n)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(1:n)}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t^{\prime}}^{(1:n)}\right]\right|^{2+\alpha}

Above, by slight abuse of notation, if
t^{\prime}=0
, we use
\mathcal{H}_{-1}^{(1:n)}\triangleq\emptyset
and
S_{0}^{(1:n)}\triangleq\emptyset
.

=\left|\sum_{s=1}^{T}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[X_{s}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(1:n)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(1:n)}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{s}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t^{\prime}}^{(1:n)}\right]\right\}\right|^{2+\alpha}

By repeatedly applying Lemma
B.2
(Inequality Using Binomial Theorem) for any numbers
a_{1},a_{2},...,a_{K}
,
|\sum_{k=1}^{K}a_{k}|^{\eta}\leq c_{2+\alpha}^{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}|a_{k}|^{2+\alpha}
for some constant
c_{2+\alpha}>0
.

\leq c_{2+\alpha}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\bigg\{\bigg|\mathbb{E}\left[X_{s}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(1:n)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(1:n)}\right]\bigg|^{2+\alpha}+\bigg|\mathbb{E}\left[X_{s}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t^{\prime}}^{(1:n)}\right]\bigg|^{2+\alpha}\bigg\}

By Jensen’s Inequality,

\leq c_{2+\alpha}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(1:n)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(1:n)}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t^{\prime}}^{(1:n)}\right]\bigg|\bigg\}

Thus, we can upper-bound display (
D.14
) as follows:

\frac{1}{n(\epsilon\sqrt{n})^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big|Z_{0}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n(\epsilon\sqrt{n})^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big|Z_{t}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]

\leq\frac{c_{2+\alpha}^{T}}{n(\epsilon\sqrt{n})^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\big|\mathcal{H}_{0}^{(1:n)},S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]\bigg]\bigg\}\\
+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{c_{2+\alpha}^{T}}{n(\epsilon\sqrt{n})^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
+\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\bigg]\bigg\}

By the law of itereated expectations,

=\frac{c_{2+\alpha}^{T}}{n(\epsilon\sqrt{n})^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{s=1}^{T}2\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]\\
+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{c_{2+\alpha}^{T}}{n(\epsilon\sqrt{n})^{\alpha}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{s=1}^{T}2\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]

To show that the above is
o_{P}(1)
, it is sufficient to show that
\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]
for all
t,s\in[1\colon T]
are all
O_{P}(1)
. By Lemma
D.3
, it is sufficient to show that
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:s}^{*}}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]
and
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\right|\right]
are bounded.

By Jensen’s inequality,

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\bigg|\mathbb{E}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\bigg|\bigg]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\right].

Thus, it is sufficient to show that
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]<\infty
for all
s\in[1\colon T]
.

By Condition
2
,
W_{2:s}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\leq\pi_{\min}^{-(s-1)}
a.s. so,

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:s}^{*}}\left[\big|X_{s}^{(i)}\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:s}^{*}}\left[\big|W_{2:s}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]

\leq\pi_{\min}^{-(s-1)(2+\alpha)}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:s}^{*}}\left[W_{2:s}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\big|f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]

=\pi_{\min}^{-(s-1)(2+\alpha)}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:s}^{*}}\left[\big|f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]<\infty.

(i) Convergence of conditional variance; Display (
D.12
):.

We now show that display (
D.12
) holds.
Using the definition of
Z_{t}^{(i)}
,

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(Z_{0}^{(i)}\big)^{2}\right]+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[\big(Z_{t}^{(i)}\big)^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg(\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{0}^{(1:n)},S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\right]\bigg)^{2}\bigg]\\
+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg(\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\bigg)^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\bigg].

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\right]^{2}\bigg\}\\
+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\bigg]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg\}.

(D.15)

Note by re-indexing,
-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}=-\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\\
=-\sum_{t=1}^{T}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}}_{(a)}-\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}}_{(b)}+\underbrace{\big(X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big)^{2}}_{(c)}
.
By rearranging terms the terms in display (
D.15
),

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\underbrace{\big(X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big)^{2}}_{(c)}-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\right]^{2}\bigg\}\\
+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg]-\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}}_{(b)}\bigg\}\\
+\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\bigg]-\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}}_{(a)}\bigg\}.

(D.16)

For the remainder of the proof we will show the following results, which combined with display (
D.16
) above are sufficient for display (
D.12
):

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\big(X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big)^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\right]^{2}\bigg\}\overset{P}{\to}\textnormal{Var}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\bigg(\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg)

(D.17)

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg\}\overset{P}{\to}0

(D.18)

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\bigg]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg\}\overset{P}{\to}0

(D.19)

Before showing the above three results, first note the following observations:

•

By Condition
2
(Minimum Exploration),

\hat{\pi}_{t}^{(n)}(A_{t}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)})\geq\pi_{\min}~~~\textnormal{a.s.}~~~~~~\textnormal{and}~~~~~~W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\leq\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}~~~\textnormal{a.s.}

(D.20)

•

Additionally note that

\left|W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta_{t-1}^{*},\hat{\beta}_{t-1}^{(n)})-1\right|=\left|W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta_{t-1}^{*},\hat{\beta}_{t-1}^{(n)})-W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta_{t-1}^{*},\beta_{t-1}^{*})\right|\\
\leq\left|\hat{\pi}_{t}^{(n)}\big(A_{t}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\big)^{-1}-\pi_{t}^{*}\big(A_{t}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\big)^{-1}\right|\leq\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left|\hat{\pi}_{t}^{(n)}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)^{-1}-\pi_{t}^{*}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)^{-1}\right|\\
\underbrace{\leq}_{(i)}\pi_{\min}^{-2}\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\left|\hat{\pi}_{t}^{(n)}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)-\pi_{t}^{*}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)\right|\underbrace{\leq}_{(ii)}\pi_{\min}^{-2}\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big\|\hat{\beta}_{t-1}^{(n)}-\beta_{t-1}^{*}\big\|_{2}.

(D.21)

Inequality (i) above holds because by Taylor Series expansion,
\hat{\pi}^{-1}-\pi^{*,-1}=(-1)\tilde{\pi}^{-2}(\hat{\pi}-\pi^{*})
for some
\tilde{\pi}
between
\hat{\pi}
and
\pi^{*}
. By Condition
2
(Minimum Exploration) and display (
D.20
),
\tilde{\pi}\geq\min(\hat{\pi},\pi^{*})\geq\pi_{\min}>0
a.s.

Inequality (ii) holds by Condition
3
(Lipschitz Policy Functions).

•

Since the action space
\mathcal{A}
is finite
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big]\leq\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big]<\infty
; the last inequality holds by Condition
3
. Thus, by Lemma
D.3
,
\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)=O_{P}(1)
.
Also since
\big\|\hat{\beta}_{t-1}^{(n)}-\beta_{t-1}^{*}\big\|_{2}=O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
by assumption, by display (
D.21
), we have that
W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})=1+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
.
Moreover, note that

W_{2:t}^{(i)}\big(\beta_{t-1}^{*},\hat{\beta}_{t-1}^{(n)}\big)=\left(1+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})\right)^{t-1}=1+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n}).

(D.22)

1. Showing Display (
D.17
) holds.

Since
X_{1:T}^{(i)}=\sum_{t=1}^{T}X_{t}^{(i)}=\sum_{t=1}^{T}W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})
,

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big)^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg\}^{2}

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})W_{2:s}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}W_{2:\max(t,s)}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})W_{2:\min(t,s)}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})

Note that since
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\big]
and
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:s}^{*}}\big[f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})^{2}\big]
is bounded by assumption, by Lemma
D.3
,
f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})=O_{P}(1)
and
f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})=O_{P}(1)
.

Moreover, by display (
D.22
), we have that
W_{2:\min(t,s)}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})=1+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
and
W_{2:\max(t,s)}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})=1+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
.

Thus,
W_{2:\max(t,s)}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})W_{2:\min(t,s)}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\\
=W_{2:\max(t,s)}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
. So,

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\bigg\{\sum_{s=1}^{T}W_{2:\max(t,s)}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})\bigg\}

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}W_{2:\max(t,s)}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})+o_{P}(1).

Note that by display (
D.20
),

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\big|W_{2:\max(t,s)}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha/2}\right]

\leq\pi_{\min}^{-\{\max(t,s)-1\}}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\big|f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha/2}\right]

\leq\pi_{\min}^{-\{\max(t,s)-1\}}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\bigg[\max\left\{\big|f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2},~\big|f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\right\}^{1+\alpha/2}\bigg]

\leq\pi_{\min}^{-\{\max(t,s)-1\}}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\left\{\big|f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2}+\big|f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\right\}^{1+\alpha/2}\right]

By Lemma
B.2
, for some positive constant
c_{1+\alpha/2}
,

\leq\pi_{\min}^{-\{\max(t,s)-1\}}c_{1+\alpha/2}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\big|f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}+\big|f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]<\infty.

The above is bounded because of our assumption that
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\big[\big|f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\big]<\infty
and

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\big[\big|f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\big]<\infty
.

Thus, we can apply the Weighted Martingale Triangular Array Weak Law of Large Numbers (Theorem
D.1
) to get that

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big)^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}W_{2:\max(t,s)}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})+o_{P}(1)\\
\overset{P}{\to}\sum_{t=1}^{T}\sum_{s=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})f_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)})\right]=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\bigg[\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg\}^{2}\bigg]

Additionally, note that

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\right]^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg]^{2}=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg]^{2}.

Thus,

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\big(X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big)^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\right]^{2}\right\}\overset{P}{\to}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\bigg[\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg\}^{2}\bigg]-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\bigg[\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg]^{2}\\
=\textnormal{Var}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\bigg(\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg).

The last equality above holds since
\textnormal{Var}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left(\sum_{t=1}^{T}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right)=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\left\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right\}^{2}\right]-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right]^{2}
.

2. Showing Display (
D.18
) holds.

Note that
\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\big[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\big]^{2}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\sum_{t=1}^{T}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|S_{1}^{(i)}\right]^{2}\right]
. Also, note the following:

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}\left[W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]\bigg\}^{2}

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]\bigg\}^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|S_{1}^{(i)}\right]\bigg\}^{2}

Since
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\big]<\infty
by assumption, thus by Lemma
B.2
,

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big\}^{2}\big]<\infty
. Since
S_{1}^{(1)},S_{1}^{(2)},S_{1}^{(3)},\dots S_{1}^{(n)}
are i.i.d., by the weak law of large numbers,

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg(\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|S_{1}^{(i)}\right]\bigg\}^{2}-\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg\{\sum_{t=1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|S_{1}^{(i)}\right]\bigg\}^{2}\bigg]\bigg)\overset{P}{\to}0

Thus, we have that

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|S_{1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg\}\overset{P}{\to}0.

3. Showing Display (
D.19
) holds.

Note that for any
t\in[1\colon T]
,

\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}X_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)}+\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}X_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)}\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\bigg]^{2}

Recall
X_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)}\triangleq W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})
.

=\bigg\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})+\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\bigg]\bigg\}^{2}

=\bigg\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\\
+W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\mathbb{E}\bigg[\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}W_{t+1:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\bigg]\bigg\}^{2}

=\bigg\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})+W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\bigg[\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\bigg]\bigg\}^{2}

Note
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\left[\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\left[\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)}\right]
, since in the expectation actions are selected using fixed target policies
\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}
(the first expectation conditions on
\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}
and the second conditions on
\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)}
).

=\bigg\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})+W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\bigg[\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)}\bigg]\bigg\}^{2}

For convenience, let
\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\triangleq f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})
for all
t^{\prime}\in[1\colon t-1]
and let
\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\triangleq f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})+\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\left[\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)}\right]
.

=\bigg\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\bigg\}^{2}

=\underbrace{\bigg\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\bigg\}^{2}}_{\triangleq(U_{1:t-1}^{(i)})^{2}}+\underbrace{\bigg\{W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\bigg\}^{2}}_{\triangleq(U_{t}^{(i)})^{2}}\\
+2\underbrace{\bigg\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\bigg\}W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})}_{\triangleq U_{1:t-1}^{(i)}U_{t}^{(i)}}

(D.23)

Using the above result to rewrite
\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}
, we can rewrite the left-hand side of display (
D.19
):

\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\bigg[\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\bigg]-\mathbb{E}\left[X_{1:T}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(1:n)},S_{t+1}^{(1:n)}\right]^{2}\bigg\}

=\sum_{t=1}^{T}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\left[(U_{1:t-1}^{(i)})^{2}+(U_{t}^{(i)})^{2}+2U_{1:t-1}U_{t}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
-(U_{1:t-1}^{(i)})^{2}-(U_{t}^{(i)})^{2}-2U_{1:t-1}^{(i)}U_{t}^{(i)}\bigg\}

Note that
(U_{1:t-1}^{(i)})^{2}
is a constant given
\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}
and cancel out in the display above. Thus,

=\frac{1}{n}2\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\left[U_{1:t-1}^{(i)}U_{t}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]-U_{1:t-1}^{(i)}U_{t}^{(i)}\bigg\}\\
+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\left[(U_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]-(U_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\bigg\}

(D.24)

Before we show that display (
D.24
) converges in probability to zero, first note the following useful results:

•

We first show that for all
t^{\prime}\in[1\colon T]
,

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t^{\prime}}^{*}}\left[\big|\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]<\infty.

(D.25)

For
t^{\prime}\in[1\colon t-1]
,
\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})=f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})
. Since
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t^{\prime}}^{*}}\big[|f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})|^{2+\alpha}\big]<\infty
by assumption.

For the
t^{\prime}=t
case, recall
\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})=f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})+\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\left[f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)}\right]
. By repeatedly applying Lemma
B.2
(Inequality Using Binomial Theorem) for any numbers
a_{1},a_{2},...,a_{K}
,
|\sum_{k=1}^{K}a_{k}|^{2+\alpha}\leq c_{2+\alpha}^{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}|a_{k}|^{2+\alpha}
for some constant
c_{2+\alpha}>0
.
Thus,

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\bigg|f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})+\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\left[f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)}\right]\bigg|^{2+\alpha}\bigg]\\
\leq c_{2+\alpha}^{T-t}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\big|f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}+\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}\bigg|\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\left[f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)}\right]\bigg|^{2+\alpha}\bigg]\\
\leq c_{2+\alpha}^{T-t}\sum_{t^{\prime}=t}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\big|f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]<\infty.

The second to last inequality above holds by Jensen’s inequality. The last inequality holds since
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t^{\prime}}^{*}}\big[|f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})|^{2+\alpha}\big]<\infty
by assumption.

•

We now show that for all
t^{\prime},s\in[1\colon T]
,

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\big|\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\tilde{f}_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)},S_{s+1}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha/2}\right]<\infty.

(D.26)

Note that for any real numbers
a,b
, that
ab\leq\frac{1}{2}(a^{2}+b^{2})
. Thus,

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\big|\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\tilde{f}_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)},S_{s+1}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha/2}\right]\\
\leq\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\big|\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}+\big|\tilde{f}_{s}(\mathcal{H}_{s}^{(i)},S_{s+1}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]<\infty.

The last inequality holds by display (
D.25
).

We now show that
\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})=O_{P}(1)
.

•

For
t^{\prime}\in[1\colon t-1]
,
\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})=f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})
by definition. Since
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t^{\prime}}^{*}}\big[|f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})|\big]<\infty
by assumption, by Lemma
D.3
,
\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})=f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})=O_{P}(1)
.

•

Recall that
\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})=f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})+\sum_{t^{\prime}=t+1}^{T}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\left[f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)}\right]
by definition.
By Lemma
D.3
,
f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})=O_{P}(1)
since
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[|f_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})|\big]<\infty
by assumption.
Also note that by Jensen’s inequality,

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\bigg|\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\left[f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)}\right]\bigg|\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\left[\big|f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)}\right]\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:T}^{*}}\left[\big|f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|\right]<\infty.

Thus by Lemma
D.3
, we have that
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t+1:T}^{*}}\left[f_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)}\right]=O_{P}(1)
.
Combining the above results we have that
\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})=O_{P}(1)
.

3a. First Summation in Display (
D.24
)

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\left[U_{1:t-1}^{(i)}U_{t}^{(i)}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]-U_{1:t-1}^{(i)}U_{t}^{(i)}\bigg\}

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg(\mathbb{E}\bigg[\bigg\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\bigg\}W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\bigg]\\
-\bigg\{\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\bigg\}W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\bigg)

=\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg(\mathbb{E}\bigg[W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\bigg]\\
-W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\bigg)

•

Note the following for
t^{\prime}\in[1\colon t-1]
:

\mathbb{E}\left[W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
\underbrace{=}_{(a)}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})W_{2:t-1}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\mathbb{E}\left[W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
\underbrace{=}_{(b)}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})W_{2:t-1}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
\underbrace{=}_{(c)}W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})W_{2:t-1}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\right]

(D.27)

•

Equality (a) above holds since
t^{\prime}<t
, so
W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})
and
\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})
are constants given
\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)}
.

•

Equality (b) above holds since the Radon-Nikodym weighting changes the policy with which actions are chosen with in the expectation.

•

Regarding equality (c), note that in the expectation indexed by
\pi_{t}^{*}
that conditions on
\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}
, the only thing that is integrated over is the distribution of
(A_{t}^{(i)},Y_{t}^{(i)})
. Given
\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}
, when actions are selected using the policy
\pi_{t}^{*}
, the distribution of
(A_{t}^{(i)},Y_{t}^{(i)})
does not depend on the data of other users, i.e.,
\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(j)},S_{t}^{(j)}
for
j\not=i
.

By display (
D.27
),

=\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg(W_{2:t-1}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\right]\\
-W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\bigg)

By display (
D.25
), Jensen’s inequality, and Lemma
D.3
, we have that
\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})=O_{P}(1)
,
\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})=O_{P}(1)
and
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\right]=O_{P}(1)
.
Additionally, by display (
D.22
),
W_{2:t^{\prime}}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})=1+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
. Thus,

=o_{P}(1)+\sum_{t^{\prime}=1}^{t-1}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg(W_{2:t-1}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\right]\\
-W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\bigg)\overset{P}{\to}0.

The final limit above holds by the Weighted Martingale Triangular Array Weak Law of Large Numbers (Theorem
D.1
); note we can apply Theorem
D.1
because we assume that Condition
2
holds and because
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\big|^{1+\alpha/2}\right]<\infty
and

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}+1}^{(i)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\right]\right|^{1+\alpha/2}\right]<\infty
by display (
D.26
) and Jensen’s inequality.

3b. Second Summation in Display (
D.24
)

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}\left[(U_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]-(U_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\bigg\}

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg(\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\right\}^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
-\left\{W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})\right\}^{2}\bigg)

Since
W_{2:t-1}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})
is a constant
\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)}
and since by the Radon-Nikodym weighting,

\mathbb{E}\left[W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})^{2}\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]
,

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg(W_{2:t-1}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})^{2}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
-W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})^{2}\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\bigg).

Note the following observations:

•

By display (
D.22
),
W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})=1+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
. By display (
D.26
) and Lemma
D.3
, we have that
\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}=O_{P}(1)
. Thus,

W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})^{2}\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}=W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n}).

(D.28)

•

Additionally, for now, we take as given that

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\right]+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n}).

(D.29)

For now, we take as given that display (
D.29
) holds; we prove this at the end of this proof.

By displays (
D.28
) and (
D.29
) above,

=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg(W_{2:t-1}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\right]\\
-W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}+O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})\bigg)

=o_{P}(1)+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg(W_{2:t-1}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\right]\\
-W_{2:t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\bigg)\overset{P}{\to}0.

The final limit above holds by the Weighted Martingale Triangular Array Weak Law of Large Numbers (Theorem
D.1
); note we can apply Theorem
D.1
because we assume that Condition
2
holds and because by display (
D.26
) and Jensen’s inequality,
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|^{1+\alpha/2}\right]<\infty
and
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\left|\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\right]\right|^{1+\alpha/2}\right]<\infty
.

Now all that remains is to show that display (
D.29
) holds. We do this below:

•

Note the following:

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]+\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\big\{W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})-1\big\}\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\right]+\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\big\{W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})-1\big\}\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]

(D.30)

The first equality above holds by adding and subtracting
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]
.

The second equality above holds because the expectation
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]
only integrates over is the distribution of
(A_{t}^{(i)},Y_{t}^{(i)})
; given
\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}
, when actions are selected using the policy
\pi_{t}^{*}
, the distribution of
(A_{t}^{(i)},Y_{t}^{(i)})
does not depend on the data of other users, i.e.,
\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(j)},S_{t}^{(j)}
for
j\not=i
.

•

Now consider just the second term in the last line of display (
D.30
) above:

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\big\{W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})-1\big\}\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
\leq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\big|W_{t}^{(i)}(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)})-1\big|\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\bigg|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\\
\underbrace{\leq}_{(a)}\pi_{\min}^{-2}\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]\big\|\hat{\beta}_{t-1}^{(n)}-\beta_{t-1}^{*}\big\|_{2}\\
\underbrace{=}_{(b)}O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n}).

Above inequality (a) holds by display (
D.21
); we are able to move the terms
\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)
and
\big\|\hat{\beta}_{t-1}^{(n)}-\beta_{t-1}^{*}\big\|_{2}
out of the conditional expectation they are known given
\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}
.

Above limit (b) holds because (i)
\hat{\beta}_{t-1}^{(n)}=O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
by assumption,

(ii)
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(1:n)},S_{t}^{(1:n)}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{t}^{*}}\left[\tilde{f}_{t}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)},S_{t+1}^{(i)})^{2}\big|\mathcal{H}_{t-1}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\right]=O_{P}(1)
by display (
D.26
), Jensen’s inequality, and Lemma
D.3
, and

(iii)
\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)=O_{P}(1)
; this holds by Lemma
D.3
since

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t-1}^{*}}\left[\big|\max_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|\right]\underbrace{\leq}_{(a)}\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t-1}^{*}}\left[\big|\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|\right]\\
=|\mathcal{A}|\sum_{a\in\mathcal{A}}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t-1}^{*}}\left[|\mathcal{A}|^{-1}\big|\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|\right]\underbrace{=}_{(b)}|\mathcal{A}|\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t-1}^{*},\pi_{t}^{\textnormal{uniform}}}\left[\big|\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(A_{t}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|\right]\\
\underbrace{=}_{(c)}|\mathcal{A}|\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\frac{\pi_{t}^{\textnormal{uniform}}(A_{t}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)})}{\pi_{t}^{*}(A_{t}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)})}\big|\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(a,S_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|\right]\\
\underbrace{\leq}_{(d)}|\mathcal{A}|\pi_{\min}^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|\dot{\pi}_{t}\big(A_{t}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|\right]\underbrace{\leq}_{(e)}\infty.

Inequality (a) above holds since the action space
\mathcal{A}
is a finite set.

Equality (b) holds for
\pi_{t}^{\textnormal{uniform}}(A_{t}^{(i)},S_{t}^{(i)})\triangleq|\mathcal{A}|^{-1}
, i.e., the policy that selects action uniformly over the action space
\mathcal{A}
for the
t^{\textnormal{th}}
action.

Equality (c) uses Radon-Nikodym derivative weights.

Inequality (d) above holds by exploration Condition
2
.

Inequality (e) holds by Condition
3
.
\blacksquare

D.5
Functional Asymptotic Normality under Finite Bracketing Integral (Theorem
D.5
)

Theorem D.5

(Functional Asymptotic Normality under Finite Bracketing Integral for Adaptively Sampled Data)
.

Let
\mathcal{F}
be any class of real-valued measurable functions of
\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}
such that for all
f\in\mathcal{F}
, for some
\alpha>0
,
\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\big(\epsilon,\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)}d\epsilon<\infty
.
Let Conditions
2
(Minimum Exploration) and
3
(Lipschitz Policy Functions) hold and also let
\hat{\beta}_{t^{\prime}}^{(n)}-\beta_{t^{\prime}}^{*}=O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
for all
t^{\prime}\in[1\colon t-1]
.
Then for

\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f)\triangleq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\big\{\hat{\pi}_{2:t}^{(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}\big[\big\{\hat{\pi}_{2:t}^{(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]\right),

the empirical process
\big\{\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f):f\in\mathcal{F}\big\}
converges in distribution to
\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}
a mean-zero Gaussian process in
\ell^{\infty}(\mathcal{F})
(the collection of all bounded functions from
\mathcal{F}
to ) with the following covariance function:

\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f)\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(g)\big]\triangleq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\left(\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})]\right)\\
\left(\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]\right)\bigg].

(D.31)

Above we use
\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\triangleq\prod_{t^{\prime}=2}^{t}\pi_{t^{\prime}}^{*}(A_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)},S_{t^{\prime}}^{(i)})
.

Proof of Theorem
D.5
.
By
[
Van der Vaart 2000
, Theorem 18.14]
, to show the desired result it is sufficient to show that the following two properties hold:

(a)

Joint Convergence of Marginals

For any finite number of functions
f_{1},f_{2},...,f_{K}\in\mathcal{F}
,

\bigg(\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f_{1}),\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f_{2}),...,\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f_{K})\bigg)\overset{D}{\to}\bigg(\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f_{1}),\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f_{2}),...,\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f_{K})\bigg)

(b)

Asymptotically Tight

For any
\epsilon,\eta>0
, there exists a partition of
\mathcal{F}
into finitely many sets
\mathcal{F}_{1},\mathcal{F}_{2},...,\mathcal{F}_{J}
such that

\limsup_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{P}^{*}\bigg(\sup_{j\in[1\colon J]}\sup_{f,f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f)-\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f^{\prime})\right|>\epsilon\bigg)\leq\eta.

Showing (a) Joint Convergence of Marginals.

We can show that (a) above holds for the stochastic process
\big\{\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f):f\in\mathcal{F}\big\}
by the Theorem
D.4
(Weighted Martingale Triangular Array Central Limit Theorem).

Specifically, by Cramer Wold device, it is sufficient to show that for any
c=[c_{1},c_{2},\dots,c_{K}]\in\real^{K}
that

\sum_{k=1}^{K}c_{k}\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f_{k})\overset{D}{\to}\mathcal{N}\left(0,c^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Sigma_{1,1}&\Sigma_{1,2}&\dots&\Sigma_{1,K}\\
\Sigma_{2,1}&\Sigma_{2,2}&\dots&\Sigma_{2,K}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
\Sigma_{K,1}&\Sigma_{K,2}&\dots&\Sigma_{K,K}\\
\end{bmatrix}c\right)

where
\Sigma_{k,k^{\prime}}\triangleq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f_{k})\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f_{k^{\prime}})\right]
.

Note that

\sum_{k=1}^{K}c_{k}\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f_{k})=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg(\big\{\hat{\pi}_{2:t}^{(i)}\big\}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}\bigg[\big\{\hat{\pi}_{2:t}^{(i)}\big\}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg]\bigg)

=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bigg(W_{2:t}^{(i)}\big(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)}\big)\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\\
-\mathbb{E}\bigg[W_{2:t}^{(i)}\big(\beta^{*},\hat{\beta}^{(n)}\big)\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg]\bigg)\overset{D}{\to}\mathcal{N}\left(0,\bar{\Sigma}\right),

where

\bar{\Sigma}\triangleq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\bigg(\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg)^{2}\bigg]-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg]^{2}.

(D.32)

The above weak convergence holds by Theorem
D.4
(Weighted Martingale Triangular Array Central Limit Theorem).
When applying Theorem
D.4
, we use the following properties:

•

Conditions
2
(Minimum Exploration) and
3
(Lipschitz Policy Functions) hold.

•

\hat{\beta}_{t^{\prime}}^{(n)}-\beta_{t^{\prime}}^{*}=O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
for all
t^{\prime}\in[1\colon t-1]
.

•

Recall that for some
\alpha>0
,
\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\big(\epsilon,\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)}d\epsilon<\infty
. This means that for all
f\in\mathcal{F}
, we can find a bracket
(l_{j},u_{j})
such that
l_{j}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq u_{j}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})
a.s. Additionally, recall that the brackets are such that
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|l\big(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|^{2+\alpha}\big]<\infty
and
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|u\big(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|^{2+\alpha}\big]<\infty
.
Thus, for all
f\in\mathcal{F}
,

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|f\big(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|^{2+\alpha}\big]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|l\big(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|^{2+\alpha}\big]+\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|u\big(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|^{2+\alpha}\big]<\infty.

Thus, by repeatedly applying Lemma
B.2
, for some positive constant
c_{2+\alpha}<\infty
, the following result holds:

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\bigg|\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg|^{2+\alpha}\bigg]\leq c_{2+\alpha}^{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\bigg|\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg|^{2+\alpha}\bigg]\\
\leq c_{2+\alpha}^{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\pi_{\min}^{(t-1)(2+\alpha)}|c_{k}|^{2+\alpha}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]<\infty.

The last inequality above holds by Condition
2
.

By the definition of
\bar{\Sigma}
from display (
D.32
),

\bar{\Sigma}=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\bigg(\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg)^{2}\bigg]-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg]^{2}

=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}\bigg\{\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}c_{k^{\prime}}f_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\bigg]\\
-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}c_{k}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right]\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}c_{k^{\prime}}f_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right]\bigg\}

=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}c_{k}c_{k^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\bigg(\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right]\bigg)\\
\bigg(\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\right]\bigg)\bigg]

By the definition of
\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f_{k})\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f_{k^{\prime}})\big]
from display (
D.31
),

=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}c_{k}c_{k^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f_{k})\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f_{k^{\prime}})\big]=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{K}c_{k}c_{k^{\prime}}\Sigma_{k,k^{\prime}}=c^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Sigma_{1,1}&\Sigma_{1,2}&\dots&\Sigma_{1,K}\\
\Sigma_{2,1}&\Sigma_{2,2}&\dots&\Sigma_{2,K}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
\Sigma_{K,1}&\Sigma_{K,2}&\dots&\Sigma_{K,K}\\
\end{bmatrix}c.

The second equality above holds since recall
\Sigma_{k,k^{\prime}}\triangleq\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f_{k})\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f_{k^{\prime}})\big]
.

Thus, we have shown that
\bar{\Sigma}
from display (
D.32
) is such that

\bar{\Sigma}=c^{\top}\begin{bmatrix}\Sigma_{1,1}&\Sigma_{1,2}&\dots&\Sigma_{1,K}\\
\Sigma_{2,1}&\Sigma_{2,2}&\dots&\Sigma_{2,K}\\
\vdots&\vdots&\ddots&\vdots\\
\Sigma_{K,1}&\Sigma_{K,2}&\dots&\Sigma_{K,K}\\
\end{bmatrix}c.

Showing (b) Asymptotically Tight.

The asymptotically tight condition above holds by the same argument used in the proof of Theorem 19.5 from
Van der Vaart 2000
, but by replacing the use of maximal inequality Lemma 19.34 of
Van der Vaart 2000
in that proof with our maximal inequality from Lemma
E.4
(Maximal Inequality as a Function of the Bracketing Integral). We discuss this argument below.

Let
\epsilon,\delta>0
. Since
\mathcal{F}
has finite bracketing integral by assumption, we can find a partition of
\mathcal{F}
into finitely many sets
\mathcal{F}_{1},\mathcal{F}_{2},...,\mathcal{F}_{N_{\delta_{0}}}
where
N_{\delta_{0}}\triangleq N_{[~]}\big(\delta_{0},\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)
and
\delta_{0}\triangleq\delta\sqrt{\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}}
. Note that

\mathbb{P}^{*}\bigg(\sup_{j\in[1\colon N_{\delta_{0}}]}\sup_{f,f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f)-\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f^{\prime})\right|>\epsilon\bigg)

Above
\mathbb{P}^{*}
refers to outer probabilities as defined in Section 18.2
Van der Vaart 2000
.

=\mathbb{P}^{*}\bigg(\sup_{j\in[1\colon N_{\delta_{0}}]}\sup_{f,f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f-f^{\prime})\right|>\epsilon\bigg)

By Markov inequality,

\leq\frac{1}{\epsilon}\mathbb{E}^{*}\bigg[\sup_{j\in[1\colon N_{\delta_{0}}]}\sup_{f,f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f-f^{\prime})\right|\bigg].

Let
\mathcal{G}_{\delta}
be the function class such that
\mathcal{G}_{\delta}\triangleq\big\{f-f^{\prime}\textnormal{~~s.t.~~}f,f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}_{j},j\in[1\colon N_{\delta_{0}}]\big\}
. Note the following observations:

•

Note that for any
f,f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}_{j}
,
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-f^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big\}^{2}\right]\leq\delta_{0}^{2}
; thus
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\big]\leq\delta_{0}^{2}
for any
g\in\mathcal{G}_{\delta}
. Note that by Condition
2
, this implies that
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\right]\leq\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\right]\leq\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}\delta_{0}^{2}=\delta^{2}
for all
g\in\mathcal{G}_{\delta}

•

We take as given for now that

\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\big(\epsilon,\mathcal{G}_{\delta},L_{2}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)}d\epsilon<\infty.

(D.33)

We show the above holds at the end of this proof.

•

We also take as given that there exists a non-negative envelope function
G
where
\big|g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|\leq G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})
a.s. for all
g\in\mathcal{G}_{\delta}
and
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\right]<\infty
(we show this at the end of this proof).

Using the above observations and Condition
2
, we can apply Lemma
E.4
(Maximal Inequality as a Function of the Bracketing Integral) to get that

\lesssim\frac{1}{\epsilon}\bigg\{\int_{0}^{\delta}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\big(\eta,\mathcal{G}_{\delta},L_{2}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)}d\eta+\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\mathbb{I}_{G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})>\sqrt{n}a(\delta)}\right]\bigg\},

(D.34)

where
a(\delta)\triangleq\delta/\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\big(\delta,\mathcal{G}_{\delta},L_{2}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)}
. Above
\lesssim
means less than or equal to when scaled by universal positive constants.

•

By display (
D.33
), the first term (integral term) in display (
D.34
) converges to zero as
\delta\to 0
.

•

Regarding the second term in display (
D.34
), since
\mathbb{I}_{G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})>\sqrt{n}a(\delta)}=1
implies that
G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big\{\sqrt{n}a(\delta)\big\}^{-1}>1
, thus,
\big|G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big\{\sqrt{n}a(\delta)\big\}^{-1}\big|\geq\mathbb{I}_{G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})>\sqrt{n}a(\delta)}
.

\sqrt{n}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\mathbb{I}_{G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})>\sqrt{n}a(\delta)}\right]\leq a(\delta)^{-1}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\right]\\
\leq a(\delta)^{-1}\pi_{\min}^{-(t-1)}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\right].

The last inequality above holds by Condition
2
.
The above goes to zero as
n\to\infty
for every fixed
\delta
.

Thus, we have that display (
D.34
) converges to zero; this is sufficient for the Theorem to hold.
All that remains is to show that display (
D.33
) holds and that we can find an envelope function
G
. We do this below.

Bracketing Functions for
\mathcal{G}_{\delta}
; display (
D.33
).

Let
\eta>0
. Since
\mathcal{F}
has finite bracketing integral by assumption, we can find
N_{\eta}\triangleq N_{[~]}\big(\eta,\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\big)
bracketing functions
\big\{(l_{k},u_{k})\big\}_{k=1}^{N_{\eta}}
. Note that for any
g\in\mathcal{G}_{\delta}
, we can find some
f,f^{\prime}\in\mathcal{F}
such that
g=f-f^{\prime}
.

We will show that the brackets
\big\{(l_{k}-u_{k^{\prime}},~u_{k}-l_{k^{\prime}})\big\}_{k=1;k^{\prime}=1}^{k=N_{\eta};k^{\prime}=N_{\eta}}
will cover
\mathcal{G}_{\delta}
and be of size
2\sqrt{c_{2}}\eta
in
L_{2+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})
norm for a positive constant
c_{2}<\infty
.

Covering:
We can find brackets
(l_{k},u_{k})
and
(l_{k^{\prime}},u_{k^{\prime}})
such that
l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})
a.s. and
l_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq f^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq u_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})
a.s. Thus,
l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-u_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-f^{\prime}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\leq u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})
a.s.

Size:
Note that

\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})+u_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\big]}

By Lemma
B.2
for some positive constant
c_{2}<\infty
,

\leq\sqrt{c_{2}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\big]+c_{2}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|u_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\big]}

Since
\sqrt{a+b}\leq\sqrt{a}+\sqrt{b}
for
a,b>0
(to see this square both sides),

\leq\sqrt{c_{2}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\big]}+\sqrt{c_{2}\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|u_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k^{\prime}}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\big]}

\leq 2\sqrt{c_{2}}\eta.

We now discuss why the last inequality above holds. By construction of our bracketing functions,
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\big]^{1/(2+\alpha)}\leq\eta
. Note that
h(x)=x^{1+\alpha/2}
is convex. By Jensen’s inequality,

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\right]^{1+\alpha/2}=h\left(\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\big]\right)\\
\leq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[h(\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\big)\right]=\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\right].

Thus, we have that

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]^{1/(1+\alpha/2)}

By taking the square root of both sides,

\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2}\right]^{1/2}\leq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|u_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-l_{k}(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big|^{2+\alpha}\right]^{1/(2+\alpha)}\leq\eta.

Bracketing Number:
By the above results we have that

N_{[~]}\left(2\sqrt{c_{2}}\eta,\mathcal{G}_{\delta},L_{2}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\right)\leq N_{[~]}\left(\eta,\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\right)^{2}.

Moreover,

N_{[~]}\big(\eta,\mathcal{G},L_{2}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)\leq N_{[~]}\big(\eta/(2\sqrt{c_{2}}),\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)^{2}.

Thus,

\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\big(\eta,\mathcal{G},L_{2}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)}d\eta\leq\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\big(\eta/(2\sqrt{c_{2}}),\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)^{2}}d\eta

By exponent property of
\log
,

=\sqrt{2}\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\big(\eta/(2\sqrt{c_{2}}),\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)}d\eta

We now use integration by substitution, with
u=\eta/(2\sqrt{c_{2}})
; note that
\frac{\partial{u}}{\partial{\eta}}=(2\sqrt{c_{2}})^{-1}
.

=\sqrt{2}(2\sqrt{c_{2}})\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\big(\eta/(2\sqrt{c_{2}}),\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)}(2\sqrt{c_{2}})^{-1}d\eta

=\sqrt{2}(2\sqrt{c_{2}})\int_{0}^{(2\sqrt{c_{2}})^{-1}}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\big(u,\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)}du<\infty.

The above is bounded by our assumption that
\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\big(u,\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)}du
. Note that if
(2\sqrt{c_{2}})^{-1}\leq 1
, the result above holds directly by this assumption. If
(2\sqrt{c_{2}})^{-1}>1
, the result above holds because
N_{[~]}\big(\eta,\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)\leq N_{[~]}\big(1,\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)\big)
for all
\eta>1
.

Envelope Function for
\mathcal{G}_{\delta}
:
We can construct the envelope function for
\mathcal{G}_{\delta}
using the brackets for
\mathcal{G}_{\delta}
that we constructed above. Specifically, the envelope function
G
can be taken to be the supremum of the upper and lower bracketing functions for
\mathcal{G}_{\delta}
. This envelope function will be such that
\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[G(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})^{2}\right]<\infty
since the brackets we constructed for
\mathcal{G}_{\delta}
have finite
L_{2+\alpha}\big(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}}\big)
norm (see Section
A.4.1
for more on the definition of bracketing functions we use).
\blacksquare

D.6
Stochastic Equicontinuity (Lemma
D.6
)

Lemma D.6

(Stochastic Equicontinuity)
.

Let
t\in[1\colon T-1]
and let
\mathcal{F}
be a class of real-valued, measurable functions of
\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}
such that
\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\left(\epsilon,\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\right)}d\epsilon<\infty
for some constant
\alpha>0
. Let
\hat{f}^{(n)}\in\mathcal{F}
be a sequence of functions such that
\nu\big(\hat{f}^{(n)},f_{0}\big)\overset{P}{\to}0
for some
f_{0}\in\mathcal{F}
where
\nu(f,g)\triangleq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|f\big(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}\big)-g\big(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)}\big)\big|^{2}\right]^{1/2}
.

Under Conditions
2
and
3
, and the condition that
\hat{\beta}_{1:t-1}^{(n)}-\beta_{1:t-1}^{*}=O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
we have that

\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}\big(\hat{f}^{(n)}\big)-\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}\big(f_{0}\big)\overset{P}{\to}0

(D.35)

where

\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f)\triangleq\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left(\big\{\hat{\pi}_{2:t}^{(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}\big[\big\{\hat{\pi}_{2:t}^{(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]\right).

Proof of Lemma
D.6
.

We use an argument akin to that of Lemma 19.24 of
Van Der Vaart and Wellner 1996
, which is for i.i.d. data. We use
\ell^{\infty}(\mathcal{F})
to refer to the collection of all bounded functions from
\mathcal{F}
to .

By Theorem
D.5
(Functional Asymptotic Normality under Finite Bracketing Integral), the following stochastic process converges weakly to a mean-zero Gaussian Process
\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}\in\ell^{\infty}(\mathcal{F})
:

\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}\triangleq\left\{\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)}(f)\textnormal{~~s.t.~~}f\in\mathcal{F}\right\}\overset{D}{\to}\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}},

(D.36)

where the limit
\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}
has the following covariance function:

\mathbb{E}\big[\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f)\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(g)\big]\triangleq\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\bigg[\left(\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}f(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})]\right)\\
\left(\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})-\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\big[\big\{\pi_{2:t}^{*,(i)}\big\}^{-1}g(\mathcal{H}_{t}^{(i)})\big]\right)\bigg].

(D.37)

We are able to apply Theorem
D.5
because of our assumptions that Conditions
2
and
3
hold, and since we’ve assumed that
\int_{0}^{1}\sqrt{\log N_{[~]}\left(\epsilon,\mathcal{F},L_{2+\alpha}(\mathcal{P}_{\pi^{*}})\right)}d\epsilon<\infty
and
\hat{\beta}_{1:t-1}^{(n)}-\beta_{1:t-1}^{*}=O_{P}(1/\sqrt{n})
.

By Lemma 18.15 of
Van der Vaart 2000
, the weak convergence result from display (
D.36
) implies that the limit
\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}
can be constructed to have almost all sample paths in
\textnormal{UC}(\mathcal{F},\rho)
, the collection of all uniformly continuous functions from
\mathcal{F}
to ;
\rho
is the standard deviation semi-metric:

\rho(f,g)\triangleq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}_{\pi_{2:t}^{*}}\left[\big|\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(f)-\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}(g)\big|^{2}\right]}

For now, we take as given that
\rho\big(\hat{f}^{(n)},f_{0}\big)\overset{P}{\to}0
(we show this at the end of this proof). Thus, we have that
\hat{f}^{(n)}\overset{P}{\to}f_{0}
. By Slutsky’s theorem and the convergence result from display (
D.36
), we have that
\big(\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}}^{(n)},\hat{f}^{(n)}\big)\overset{D}{\to}\big(\mathbb{G}_{\mathcal{F}},f_{0}\big)
.
